Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The giants operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 137. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 817 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the giants space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 137 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 817. QED.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
